* During the period, the terms of the 2024 LTIP were changed such that awards granted to employees below Board level, which were previously
classified as equity-settled, will now be settled in cash. The modification took effect on 1 January 2026 and affected 12.49 million unvested
awards.. Following the modification, a cash-settled share-based payment liability was recognised in accordance with IFRS 2. The modification
resulted in a transfer of $10.2 million from share based payment reserve to share based payment liability and an incremental fair value expense of
$22.9 million, which has been recognised as share-based payment expense during the reporting period. The fair value of the share-based liability
was subsequently remeasured at 30 September 2026 using a binomial valuation model.
26. Earnings per share EPS
Basic
Basic EPS is calculated on the Group’s profit after taxation attributable to the parent entity, which is based on the weighted average number of
issued and fully paid ordinary shares during the period.
Diluted
Diluted EPS is calculated by dividing the profit after taxation attributable to the parent entity by the weighted average number of ordinary shares
outstanding during the period plus all the dilutive potential ordinary shares (arising from outstanding share awards in the share-based payment
scheme) into ordinary shares.

27. Proposed dividend
For the third quarter ended 30 September 2026, the Group’s directors declared an interim dividend of 19 cents per share, consisting of core
dividend of 5 cents per share and special dividend of 14 cents per share. (Dec 2025: a final dividend of 5 cents per share and a special dividend
of 3.3 cents per share).
28. Related party relationships and transactions
The Group is controlled by Seplat Energy Plc (the parent Company).
During the period, Heirs Energies and Heirs Holding Limited acquired 21.07% of the issued share capital of the Seplat Energy PLC. These entities
are controlled by Mr Tony O Elumelu CFR. He was appointed to the Board effective 22 January 2026.
The following are details of transactions and balances with related parties. The terms of these transactions are at arm’s length.
Entities controlled by Directors of the Group
United Bank for Africa: Mr. Tony Elumelu is a Director and shareholder of United Bank for Africa Plc (‘UBA’). UBA provides the Group with joint
venture–related banking services, financing, and direct banking support. The bank is also one of the Group’s lenders under the $400 million
revolving credit facility (‘RCF’), which remained undrawn as at the reporting date. UBA also provides a $978 thousand customs bond for
operational cargo clearance for an annual fee of 0.15%. At the end of the reporting period, the Group held balances of $90.1 million with UBA,
comprising joint venture and corporate balances.
Transcorp Power Limited: During the period, the Group engaged in gas sales transactions with Transcorp Power Limited, an entity controlled by
a Director of the Group (Mr. Tony Elumelu), amounting to $2.3 million. As at the reporting date, amounts due from Transcorp Power Limited was
$6.2 million.
Transcorp Hotel Plc: Transcorp Hotels Plc is an entity controlled by a Director of the Group (Mr. Tony Elumelu). During the period, Transcorp
Hotels Plc provided hospitality services to the Group, with services rendered amounting to $771 thousand. The Group had an outstanding payable
balance of $23 thousand with Transcorp Hotels Plc as at the reporting date.
Shareholders of the parent company
Vaaris Resources JVCo Limited: Following the sale of TotalEnergies EP Nigeria Limited’s (TEPNG) non-operated interest in the Renaissance JV
(formerly SPDC) licences to Vaaris, Seplat consented, through a Deed of Novation, to Vaaris replacing TEPNG under the ANOH Unitization and
Unit Operating Agreement (UUOA), to which the Renaissance JV is a party. Vaaris is controlled by Platform Petroleum, a shareholder of Seplat. No
transactions or outstanding balances arose from the arrangement.
Entities controlled by Directors of the Company
Vaaris Resources JVCo Limited: Mr Kazeem Raimi, a Non-Executive Director of Seplat PLC, is also a director of Vaaris, which replaced TEPNG
under the ANOH Unitization and Unit Operating Agreement (UUOA) pursuant to a Deed of Novation consented to by Seplat during the period. No
transactions or outstanding balances arose from the arrangement
29. Commitments and contingencies
29.1 Contingent liabilities
The Group is involved in a number of legal suits as defendant. The estimated value of the contingent liabilities for the period ended 30 Sept 2026
is $338.82 million, (Dec 2025: $275.75 million). The contingent liability for the year is determined based on possible occurrences, though unlikely to
occur. No provision has been made for this potential liability in these financial statements. Management and the Group’s solicitors are of the
opinion that the Group will suffer no loss from these claims.
30. Events after the reporting period
There are no other events which could have had a material effect on the financial position of the Group as at 30 September 2026 and its financial
performance for the period then ended that have not been adequately provided for or disclosed in these financial statements.
